# Project Agent Rules

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

+    const State128* __restrict__ current_frontier_states,
+    const std::uint64_t* __restrict__ parent_base,
+    const std::uint32_t* __restrict__ count,
+    Stream1TransformerNetworkView network,
+    half* __restrict__ tokens,
+    std::uint32_t b_micro) {
+    const std::uint32_t row_token = blockIdx.x;
+    const std::uint32_t dim = blockIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::uint32_t row = row_token / network.dims.seq_len;
+    const std::uint32_t token = row_token % network.dims.seq_len;
+    if (row >= b_micro || row >= *count || dim >= network.dims.d_model) {
+        return;
+    }
+    float value = 0.0f;
+    if (token == 0U) {
+        value = stream1_load_scalar_device(network.cls_token, dim, network.dims.dtype);
+    } else {
+        const std::uint32_t piece = token - 1U;
+        value = stream1_load_scalar_device(
+            network.fast_piece_static,
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(piece) * network.dims.d_model + dim,
+            network.dims.dtype);
+        const State128 state = current_frontier_states[*parent_base + row];
+        for (std::uint32_t slot = 0; slot < network.dims.max_piece_size; ++slot) {
+            const std::uint64_t piece_slot = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(piece) * network.dims.max_piece_size + slot;
+            if (network.piece_mask[piece_slot] == 0U) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const std::uint32_t pos = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(network.piece_positions[piece_slot]);
+            const std::uint32_t state_value = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(state.v[pos]);
+            const std::uint64_t table_idx =
+                ((static_cast<std::uint64_t>(slot) * network.dims.num_classes + state_value) * network.dims.d_model) + dim;
+            value += stream1_load_scalar_device(network.fast_slot_projected, table_idx, network.dims.dtype);
+        }
+    }
+    const std::uint64_t out_idx = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row_token) * network.dims.d_model + dim;
+    stream1_store_scalar_device(tokens, out_idx, value, network.dims.dtype);
+}
+
+__global__ void stream1_transformer_layernorm_copy_kernel(
+    const half* __restrict__ input,
+    half* __restrict__ output,
+    const half* __restrict__ gamma,
+    const half* __restrict__ beta,
+    std::uint32_t rows,
+    std::uint32_t cols,
+    std::uint32_t dtype) {
+    const std::uint32_t row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+    extern __shared__ float reduce[];
+    const std::uint64_t base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * cols;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < cols; col += blockDim.x) {
+        sum += stream1_load_scalar_device(input, base + col, dtype);
+    }
+    reduce[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            reduce[threadIdx.x] += reduce[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    const float mean = reduce[0] / static_cast<float>(cols);
+    float var_sum = 0.0f;
+    for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < cols; col += blockDim.x) {
+        const float x = stream1_load_scalar_device(input, base + col, dtype);
+        const float d = x - mean;
+        var_sum += d * d;
+    }
+    reduce[threadIdx.x] = var_sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            reduce[threadIdx.x] += reduce[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    const float inv_std = rsqrtf(reduce[0] / static_cast<float>(cols) + 1.0e-5f);
+    for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < cols; col += blockDim.x) {
+        const float x = stream1_load_scalar_device(input, base + col, dtype);
+        const float y = (x - mean) * inv_std *
+            stream1_load_scalar_device(gamma, col, dtype) +
+            stream1_load_scalar_device(beta, col, dtype);
+        stream1_store_scalar_device(output, base + col, y, dtype);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void stream1_transformer_residual_bias_add_kernel(
+    half* __restrict__ tokens,
+    const half* __restrict__ branch,
+    const half* __restrict__ bias,
+    std::uint32_t total_tokens,
+    std::uint32_t d_model,
+    std::uint32_t dtype) {
+    const std::uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::uint32_t total = total_tokens * d_model;
+    if (i >= total) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::uint32_t col = i % d_model;
+    const float y = stream1_load_scalar_device(tokens, i, dtype) +
+        stream1_load_scalar_device(branch, i, dtype) +
+        stream1_load_scalar_device(bias, col, dtype);
+    stream1_store_scalar_device(tokens, i, y, dtype);
+}
+
+__global__ void stream1_transformer_bias_silu_kernel(
+    half* __restrict__ matrix,
+    const half* __restrict__ bias,
+    std::uint32_t rows,
+    std::uint32_t cols,
+    std::uint32_t dtype) {
+    const std::uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::uint32_t total = rows * cols;
+    if (i >= total) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::uint32_t col = i % cols;
+    const float x = stream1_load_scalar_device(matrix, i, dtype) +
+        stream1_load_scalar_device(bias, col, dtype);
+    const float y = x / (1.0f + expf(-x));
+    stream1_store_scalar_device(matrix, i, y, dtype);
+}
+
+// Standalone tiled attention for current p900 seq_len=51. Full external FA2 integration is deferred.
+__global__ void stream1_transformer_attention51_kernel(
+    const half* __restrict__ qkv,
+    const half* __restrict__ qkv_bias,
+    float* __restrict__ scores_probs,
+    half* __restrict__ context,
+    Stream1TransformerDims dims,
+    std::uint32_t b_micro) {
+    const std::uint32_t row = blockIdx.x;
+    const std::uint32_t head = blockIdx.y;
+    const std::uint32_t query = blockIdx.z;
+    const std::uint32_t tid = threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= b_micro || head >= dims.nhead || query >= dims.seq_len) {
+        return;
+    }
+    extern __shared__ float shared[];
+    const std::uint64_t score_base =
+        (((static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.nhead + head) * dims.seq_len + query) * dims.seq_len);
+    const std::uint32_t head_offset = head * dims.head_dim;
+    const float scale = rsqrtf(static_cast<float>(dims.head_dim));
+    float score = -3.4028234663852886e38f;
+    if (tid < dims.seq_len) {
+        float dot = 0.0f;
+        for (std::uint32_t d = 0; d < dims.head_dim; ++d) {
+            const std::uint64_t q_idx =
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + query) * (3ULL * dims.d_model) + head_offset + d;
+            const std::uint64_t k_idx =
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + tid) * (3ULL * dims.d_model) + dims.d_model + head_offset + d;
+            const float q = stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv, q_idx, dims.dtype) +
+                stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv_bias, head_offset + d, dims.dtype);
+            const float k = stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv, k_idx, dims.dtype) +
+                stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv_bias, dims.d_model + head_offset + d, dims.dtype);
+            dot += q * k;
+        }
+        score = dot * scale;
+        scores_probs[score_base + tid] = score;
+    }
+    shared[tid] = score;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
+        if (tid < stride) {
+            shared[tid] = fmaxf(shared[tid], shared[tid + stride]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    const float max_score = shared[0];
+    float exp_value = 0.0f;
+    if (tid < dims.seq_len) {
+        exp_value = expf(scores_probs[score_base + tid] - max_score);
+        scores_probs[score_base + tid] = exp_value;
+    }
+    shared[tid] = exp_value;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
+        if (tid < stride) {
+            shared[tid] += shared[tid + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    const float inv_sum = 1.0f / shared[0];
+    if (tid < dims.seq_len) {
+        scores_probs[score_base + tid] *= inv_sum;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    if (tid < dims.head_dim) {
+        float acc = 0.0f;
+        for (std::uint32_t key = 0; key < dims.seq_len; ++key) {
+            const float prob = scores_probs[score_base + key];
+            const std::uint64_t v_idx =
+                (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + key) * (3ULL * dims.d_model) +
+                2ULL * dims.d_model + head_offset + tid;
+            const float v = stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv, v_idx, dims.dtype) +
+                stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv_bias, 2ULL * dims.d_model + head_offset + tid, dims.dtype);
+            acc += prob * v;
+        }
+        const std::uint64_t out_idx =
+            (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + query) * dims.d_model + head_offset + tid;
+        stream1_store_scalar_device(context, out_idx, acc, dims.dtype);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void stream1_transformer_cls_layernorm_kernel(
+    const half* __restrict__ tokens,
+    half* __restrict__ cls,
+    const half* __restrict__ gamma,
+    const half* __restrict__ beta,
+    Stream1TransformerDims dims,
+    std::uint32_t b_micro) {
+    const std::uint32_t row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= b_micro) {
+        return;
+    }
+    extern __shared__ float reduce[];
+    const std::uint64_t in_base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len * dims.d_model;
+    const std::uint64_t out_base = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.d_model;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < dims.d_model; col += blockDim.x) {
+        sum += stream1_load_scalar_device(tokens, in_base + col, dims.dtype);
+    }
+    reduce[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            reduce[threadIdx.x] += reduce[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    const float mean = reduce[0] / static_cast<float>(dims.d_model);
+    float var_sum = 0.0f;
+    for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < dims.d_model; col += blockDim.x) {
+        const float x = stream1_load_scalar_device(tokens, in_base + col, dims.dtype);
+        const float d = x - mean;
+        var_sum += d * d;
+    }
+    reduce[threadIdx.x] = var_sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            reduce[threadIdx.x] += reduce[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    const float inv_std = rsqrtf(reduce[0] / static_cast<float>(dims.d_model) + 1.0e-5f);
+    for (std::uint32_t col = threadIdx.x; col < dims.d_model; col += blockDim.x) {
+        const float x = stream1_load_scalar_device(tokens, in_base + col, dims.dtype);
+        const float y = (x - mean) * inv_std *
+            stream1_load_scalar_device(gamma, col, dims.dtype) +
+            stream1_load_scalar_device(beta, col, dims.dtype);
+        stream1_store_scalar_device(cls, out_base + col, y, dims.dtype);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void stream1_transformer_score_quantize_kernel(
+    const half* __restrict__ logits,
+    const half* __restrict__ output_bias,
+    const std::uint32_t* __restrict__ count,
+    std::uint32_t* __restrict__ score_ring,
+    std::uint32_t b_micro,
+    Stream1TransformerDims dims) {
+    const std::uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::uint32_t total = b_micro * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
+    if (i >= total) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const std::uint32_t row = i / static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
+    const std::uint32_t move = i % static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
+    if (row >= *count) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const float q = stream1_load_scalar_device(logits, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.output_dim + move, dims.dtype) +
+        stream1_load_scalar_device(output_bias, move, dims.dtype);
+    score_ring[i] = score_key_from_float_device(q);
+}
+
+void stream1_transformer_inference_cuda(
+    const State128* current_frontier_states,
+    const std::uint64_t* parent_base,
+    const std::uint32_t* count,
+    const Stream1TransformerNetworkView& network,
+    const Stream1TransformerScratchView& scratch,
+    std::uint32_t* score_ring,
+    std::uint32_t b_micro,
+    cudaStream_t stream) {
+    NvtxRange range("Stream1_transformer_inference_launch");
+    const Stream1TransformerDims dims = network.dims;
+    if (dims.output_dim != MOVE_COUNT) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer output_dim must equal MOVE_COUNT");
+    }
+    if (dims.d_model == 0U || dims.nhead == 0U || dims.head_dim == 0U ||
+        dims.d_model != dims.nhead * dims.head_dim) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer d_model must equal nhead * head_dim");
+    }
+    if (dims.seq_len != dims.num_pieces + 1U || dims.max_piece_size == 0U) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer sequence/piece dimensions are inconsistent");
+    }
+    if (dims.seq_len > 64U || dims.head_dim > 64U) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer fused attention tile requires seq_len<=64 and head_dim<=64");
+    }
+    if (dims.dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
+        int device = 0;
+        cudaGetDevice(&device);
+        cudaDeviceProp prop{};
+        cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device);
+        if (prop.major < 8) {
+            throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer bf16 path requires SM80+ GPU; use fp16 weights on T4/SM75");
+        }
+    } else if (dims.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer dtype must be fp16 or bf16");
+    }
+    if (b_micro == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+#if BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS
+    const std::uint32_t token_rows = b_micro * dims.seq_len;
+    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(scratch.tokens, 0, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(token_rows) * dims.d_model * sizeof(half), stream));
+    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(scratch.qkv, 0, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(token_rows) * 3ULL * dims.d_model * sizeof(half), stream));
+    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(scratch.attention_scores_probs, 0, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * dims.nhead * dims.seq_len * dims.seq_len * sizeof(float), stream));
+    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(scratch.attention_context, 0, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(token_rows) * dims.d_model * sizeof(half), stream));
+    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(scratch.ff_hidden, 0, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(token_rows) * dims.ff_dim * sizeof(half), stream));
+    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(scratch.logits, 0, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b_micro) * dims.output_dim * sizeof(half), stream));
+    const dim3 token_block(128);
+    const dim3 token_grid(token_rows, (dims.d_model + token_block.x - 1U) / token_block.x);
+    stream1_transformer_build_input_kernel<<<token_grid, token_block, 0, stream>>>(
+        current_frontier_states,
+        parent_base,
+        count,
+        network,
+        scratch.tokens,
+        b_micro);
+
+    stream1_transformer_layernorm_copy_kernel<<<token_rows, 256, 256 * sizeof(float), stream>>>(
+        scratch.tokens,
+        scratch.tokens,
+        network.input_ln_gamma,
+        network.input_ln_beta,
+        token_rows,
+        dims.d_model,
+        dims.dtype);
+
+    for (std::uint32_t layer = 0; layer < dims.transformer_layers; ++layer) {
+        const Stream1TransformerBlockView block = network.blocks[layer];
+        stream1_transformer_layernorm_copy_kernel<<<token_rows, 256, 256 * sizeof(float), stream>>>(
+            scratch.tokens,
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            block.ln1_gamma,
+            block.ln1_beta,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype);
+        stream1_cutlass_linear_cuda(
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            block.attn_qkv_weight,
+            scratch.qkv,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            3U * dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype,
+            stream);
+        const dim3 attn_grid(b_micro, dims.nhead, dims.seq_len);
+        stream1_transformer_attention51_kernel<<<attn_grid, 64, 64 * sizeof(float), stream>>>(
+            scratch.qkv,
+            block.attn_qkv_bias,
+            scratch.attention_scores_probs,
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            dims,
+            b_micro);
+        stream1_cutlass_linear_cuda(
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            block.attn_out_weight,
+            scratch.qkv,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype,
+            stream);
+        stream1_transformer_residual_bias_add_kernel<<<(token_rows * dims.d_model + 255U) / 256U, 256, 0, stream>>>(
+            scratch.tokens,
+            scratch.qkv,
+            block.attn_out_bias,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype);
+
+        stream1_transformer_layernorm_copy_kernel<<<token_rows, 256, 256 * sizeof(float), stream>>>(
+            scratch.tokens,
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            block.ln2_gamma,
+            block.ln2_beta,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype);
+        stream1_cutlass_linear_cuda(
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            block.ff1_weight,
+            scratch.ff_hidden,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.ff_dim,
+            dims.dtype,
+            stream);
+        stream1_transformer_bias_silu_kernel<<<(token_rows * dims.ff_dim + 255U) / 256U, 256, 0, stream>>>(
+            scratch.ff_hidden,
+            block.ff1_bias,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.ff_dim,
+            dims.dtype);
+        stream1_cutlass_linear_cuda(
+            scratch.ff_hidden,
+            block.ff2_weight,
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.ff_dim,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype,
+            stream);
+        stream1_transformer_residual_bias_add_kernel<<<(token_rows * dims.d_model + 255U) / 256U, 256, 0, stream>>>(
+            scratch.tokens,
+            scratch.attention_context,
+            block.ff2_bias,
+            token_rows,
+            dims.d_model,
+            dims.dtype);
+    }
+
+    stream1_transformer_cls_layernorm_kernel<<<b_micro, 256, 256 * sizeof(float), stream>>>(
+        scratch.tokens,
+        scratch.attention_context,
+        network.output_ln_gamma,
+        network.output_ln_beta,
+        dims,
+        b_micro);
+    stream1_cutlass_linear_cuda(
+        scratch.attention_context,
+        network.output_weight,
+        scratch.logits,
+        b_micro,
+        dims.d_model,
+        dims.output_dim,
+        dims.dtype,
+        stream);
+    stream1_transformer_score_quantize_kernel<<<(b_micro * MOVE_COUNT + 255U) / 256U, 256, 0, stream>>>(
+        scratch.logits,
+        network.output_bias,
+        count,
+        score_ring,
+        b_micro,
+        dims);
+#else
+    (void)current_frontier_states;
+    (void)parent_base;
+    (void)count;
+    (void)network;
+    (void)scratch;
+    (void)score_ring;
+    (void)b_micro;
+    (void)stream;
+    throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 piece_transformer CUDA forward requires CUTLASS-enabled build");
+#endif
+}
 void stream1_inference_custom_cuda(
     const State128* current_frontier_states,
     const std::uint64_t* parent_base,
     const std::uint32_t* count,
     const Stream1NetworkView& network,
     std::uint32_t* score_ring,
     std::uint32_t,
     std::uint32_t,
     std::uint32_t b_micro,
     cudaStream_t stream) {
     NvtxRange range("Stream1_custom_inference_launch");
     const std::size_t shared_bytes =
         (static_cast<std::size_t>(network.dims.hidden1) + static_cast<std::size_t>(network.dims.hidden2)) * sizeof(float);
     stream1_inference_custom_kernel<<<b_micro, MOVE_COUNT, shared_bytes, stream>>>(
         current_frontier_states,
         parent_base,
         count,
         network,
         score_ring,
         0,
         0,
         b_micro);
 }
 
 void stream1_inference_cutlass_cuda(
     const State128* current_frontier_states,
     const std::uint64_t* parent_base,
     const std::uint32_t* count,
     const std::uint8_t* generators,
     const Stream1NetworkView& network,
     const Stream1CutlassScratch& scratch,
     std::uint32_t* score_ring,
     std::uint32_t b_micro,
     cudaStream_t stream) {
     NvtxRange range("Stream1_CUTLASS_inference_launch");
     if (network.dims.output_dim != MOVE_COUNT &&
         network.dims.output_dim != STREAM1_SINGLE_SCORE_OUTPUT_DIM) {
         throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 output_dim must be 1 or MOVE_COUNT");
     }
     if (network.dims.output_dim == STREAM1_SINGLE_SCORE_OUTPUT_DIM && generators == nullptr) {
         throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 output_dim=1 requires generator table");
     }

+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const double value = std::strtod(text.c_str() + pos, &end);
+            if (end == text.c_str() + pos) {

+            values.push_back(value);
+            pos = static_cast<std::size_t>(end - text.c_str());

+    if (values.size() != expected_count) {

 inline ScratchAllocation alloc_stream1_scratch(
     const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
     std::uint32_t b_micro,
     std::uint32_t lane_count) {
     ScratchAllocation scratch;
     try {
         const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model) * lane_count;
         if (model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.hidden1, rows * model.hidden1 * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.hidden2, rows * model.hidden2 * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.residual, rows * model.hidden2 * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.output, rows * model.output_dim * sizeof(half)));
             return scratch;
         }
         if (model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
             const std::uint64_t token_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
             const std::uint64_t qkv_values = rows * model.seq_len * 3ULL * model.d_model;
             const std::uint64_t attention_values = rows * model.nhead * model.seq_len * model.seq_len;
             const std::uint64_t context_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
             const std::uint64_t ff_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.ff_dim;
             const std::uint64_t logits_values = rows * model.output_dim;
             // Future attention kernels can overwrite this float score buffer with softmax probabilities in place.
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_tokens, token_values * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_qkv, qkv_values * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs, attention_values * sizeof(float)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_attention_context, context_values * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_ff_hidden, ff_values * sizeof(half)));
             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_logits, logits_values * sizeof(half)));
             return scratch;
         }
         throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in scratch allocation");
     } catch (...) {
         free_stream1_scratch(scratch);
         throw;
     }
 }
 inline void free_stream1_scratch(ScratchAllocation& scratch) {
     cudaFree(scratch.hidden1);
     cudaFree(scratch.hidden2);
     cudaFree(scratch.residual);
     cudaFree(scratch.output);
     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_tokens);
     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_qkv);
     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs);
     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_attention_context);
     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_ff_hidden);
     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_logits);
     scratch = ScratchAllocation{};
 }

+        throw std::runtime_error("device transformer block count does not match manifest");
+    }
+    TransformerNetworkViewHolder holder;
+    holder.blocks.resize(weights.blocks.size());
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < weights.blocks.size(); ++i) {
+        const DeviceTransformerBlockWeights& b = weights.blocks[i];
+        holder.blocks[i] = Stream1TransformerBlockView{
+            b.ln1_gamma,
+            b.ln1_beta,
+            b.attn_qkv_weight,
+            b.attn_qkv_bias,
+            b.attn_out_weight,
+            b.attn_out_bias,
+            b.ln2_gamma,
+            b.ln2_beta,
+            b.ff1_weight,
+            b.ff1_bias,
+            b.ff2_weight,
+            b.ff2_bias};
+    }
+    holder.view = Stream1TransformerNetworkView{
+        weights.fast_slot_projected,
+        weights.fast_piece_static,
+        weights.cls_token,
+        weights.input_ln_gamma,
+        weights.input_ln_beta,
+        weights.output_ln_gamma,
+        weights.output_ln_beta,
+        holder.blocks.data(),
+        weights.output_weight,
+        weights.output_bias,
+        weights.piece_positions,
+        weights.piece_mask,
+        weights.piece_types,
+        transformer_dims(model)};
+    return holder;
+}
+
+inline void free_transformer_network_view(TransformerNetworkViewHolder& holder) {
+    holder = TransformerNetworkViewHolder{};
+}
+inline Stream1TransformerScratchView transformer_scratch_view(const ScratchAllocation& scratch) {
+    return Stream1TransformerScratchView{
+        scratch.transformer_tokens,
+        scratch.transformer_qkv,
+        scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs,
+        scratch.transformer_attention_context,
+        scratch.transformer_ff_hidden,
+        scratch.transformer_logits};
+}
 #endif
 
 } // namespace beam::stream1_weights

 template <typename Fn>
 float time_single_stream_ms(cudaStream_t stream, Fn launch) {
     cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
     cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
     launch();
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
     float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
     BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop));
     cudaEventDestroy(start);
     cudaEventDestroy(stop);
     return elapsed_ms;
 }
 
 std::vector<cudaStream_t> create_streams(std::uint32_t count) {
     std::vector<cudaStream_t> streams(count);
     for (cudaStream_t& stream : streams) {
         BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
     }
     return streams;
 }
 
 void destroy_streams(std::vector<cudaStream_t>& streams) {
     for (cudaStream_t stream : streams) {
         cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
     }
     streams.clear();
 }
 
 struct Stream1Result {
     std::uint32_t b_micro = 0;
     std::uint32_t concurrent = 0;
     float ms = 0.0f;
     double parent_per_sec = 0.0;
     double candidate_per_sec = 0.0;
 };
 
 struct StreamResult {
     std::string name;
     std::uint32_t param_a = 0;
     std::uint32_t param_b = 0;
     float ms = 0.0f;
     double candidate_per_sec = 0.0;
 };
 
 std::vector<State128> make_state_batch(
     State128 seed,
     std::uint32_t count,
     std::uint32_t num_classes) {
     std::vector<State128> states(count, seed);
     for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
         states[i].v[0] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((states[i].v[0] + i) % num_classes);
         states[i].v[STATE_LEN] = 0;
     }
     return states;
 }

  455: __global__ void stream1_single_output_quantize_kernel(
  456:     const half* residual,
  457:     const half* output_weight,
  458:     const half* output_bias,
  459:     const std::uint32_t* count,
  460:     std::uint32_t* score_ring,
  461:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  462:     std::uint32_t hidden2,
  463:     std::uint32_t dtype) {
  464:     const std::uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
  465:     const std::uint32_t total = b_micro * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
  466:     if (i >= total) {
  467:         return;
  468:     }
  469:     const std::uint32_t parent_local = i / static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);

  480:     score_ring[i] = score_key_from_float_device(q);

  483: __global__ void stream1_transformer_build_input_kernel(
  484:     const State128* __restrict__ current_frontier_states,
  485:     const std::uint64_t* __restrict__ parent_base,
  486:     const std::uint32_t* __restrict__ count,
  487:     Stream1TransformerNetworkView network,
  488:     half* __restrict__ tokens,
  489:     std::uint32_t b_micro) {
  490:     const std::uint32_t row_token = blockIdx.x;

  492:     const std::uint32_t row = row_token / network.dims.seq_len;
  493:     const std::uint32_t token = row_token % network.dims.seq_len;
  494:     if (row >= b_micro || row >= *count || dim >= network.dims.d_model) {
  495:         return;
  496:     }
  497:     float value = 0.0f;
  498:     if (token == 0U) {
  499:         value = stream1_load_scalar_device(network.cls_token, dim, network.dims.dtype);
  500:     } else {
  501:         const std::uint32_t piece = token - 1U;
  502:         value = stream1_load_scalar_device(
  503:             network.fast_piece_static,
  504:             static_cast<std::uint64_t>(piece) * network.dims.d_model + dim,
  505:             network.dims.dtype);
  506:         const State128 state = current_frontier_states[*parent_base + row];
  507:         for (std::uint32_t slot = 0; slot < network.dims.max_piece_size; ++slot) {
  508:             const std::uint64_t piece_slot = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(piece) * network.dims.max_piece_size + slot;
  509:             if (network.piece_mask[piece_slot] == 0U) {
  510:                 continue;
  511:             }
  512:             const std::uint32_t pos = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(network.piece_positions[piece_slot]);
  513:             const std::uint32_t state_value = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(state.v[pos]);
  514:             const std::uint64_t table_idx =
  515:                 ((static_cast<std::uint64_t>(slot) * network.dims.num_classes + state_value) * network.dims.d_model) + dim;
  516:             value += stream1_load_scalar_device(network.fast_slot_projected, table_idx, network.dims.dtype);
  517:         }
  518:     }
  519:     const std::uint64_t out_idx = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row_token) * network.dims.d_model + dim;
  520:     stream1_store_scalar_device(tokens, out_idx, value, network.dims.dtype);
  521: }

  612: __global__ void stream1_transformer_attention51_kernel(
  613:     const half* __restrict__ qkv,
  614:     const half* __restrict__ qkv_bias,

  628:         (((static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.nhead + head) * dims.seq_len + query) * dims.seq_len);
  629:     const std::uint32_t head_offset = head * dims.head_dim;
  630:     const float scale = rsqrtf(static_cast<float>(dims.head_dim));
  631:     float score = -3.4028234663852886e38f;

  636:                 (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + query) * (3ULL * dims.d_model) + head_offset + d;
  637:             const std::uint64_t k_idx =
  638:                 (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + tid) * (3ULL * dims.d_model) + dims.d_model + head_offset + d;

  640:                 stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv_bias, head_offset + d, dims.dtype);
  641:             const float k = stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv, k_idx, dims.dtype) +
  642:                 stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv_bias, dims.d_model + head_offset + d, dims.dtype);
  643:             dot += q * k;
  644:         }
  645:         score = dot * scale;
  646:         scores_probs[score_base + tid] = score;

  648:     shared[tid] = score;
  649:     __syncthreads();
  650:     for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {

  652:             shared[tid] = fmaxf(shared[tid], shared[tid + stride]);
  653:         }
  654:         __syncthreads();
  655:     }
  656:     const float max_score = shared[0];
  657:     float exp_value = 0.0f;
  658:     if (tid < dims.seq_len) {
  659:         exp_value = expf(scores_probs[score_base + tid] - max_score);
  660:         scores_probs[score_base + tid] = exp_value;
  661:     }
  662:     shared[tid] = exp_value;
  663:     __syncthreads();
  664:     for (std::uint32_t stride = blockDim.x / 2U; stride > 0U; stride >>= 1U) {

  666:             shared[tid] += shared[tid + stride];
  667:         }
  668:         __syncthreads();
  669:     }
  670:     const float inv_sum = 1.0f / shared[0];
  671:     if (tid < dims.seq_len) {
  672:         scores_probs[score_base + tid] *= inv_sum;
  673:     }
  674:     __syncthreads();
  675:     if (tid < dims.head_dim) {
  676:         float acc = 0.0f;
  677:         for (std::uint32_t key = 0; key < dims.seq_len; ++key) {
  678:             const float prob = scores_probs[score_base + key];
  679:             const std::uint64_t v_idx =
  680:                 (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + key) * (3ULL * dims.d_model) +
  681:                 2ULL * dims.d_model + head_offset + tid;
  682:             const float v = stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv, v_idx, dims.dtype) +
  683:                 stream1_load_scalar_device(qkv_bias, 2ULL * dims.d_model + head_offset + tid, dims.dtype);
  684:             acc += prob * v;

  686:         const std::uint64_t out_idx =
  687:             (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dims.seq_len + query) * dims.d_model + head_offset + tid;
  688:         stream1_store_scalar_device(context, out_idx, acc, dims.dtype);

  165: inline std::string read_text_exact(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
  166:     std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
  167:     if (!file) {
  168:         throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required text file: " + path.string());
  169:     }
  170:     return std::string(std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(file), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
  171: }
  172: 
  173: inline std::vector<std::byte> read_binary_exact(
  174:     const std::filesystem::path& path,
  175:     std::uint64_t expected_bytes) {
  176:     if (expected_bytes > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max())) {
  177:         throw std::runtime_error("binary expected size exceeds size_t: " + path.string());
  178:     }
  179:     std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
  180:     if (!file) {
  181:         throw std::runtime_error("cannot open required binary file: " + path.string());
  182:     }
  183:     std::vector<std::byte> bytes(static_cast<std::size_t>(expected_bytes));
  184:     file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(bytes.data()), static_cast<std::streamsize>(bytes.size()));
  185:     const std::size_t actual = static_cast<std::size_t>(file.gcount());
  186:     file.peek();
  187:     if (actual != bytes.size() || !file.eof()) {
  188:         throw std::runtime_error(
  189:             "binary file size mismatch: " + path.string() +
  190:             " expected=" + std::to_string(bytes.size()) +
  191:             " actual_read=" + std::to_string(actual));
  192:     }
  193:     return bytes;
  194: }
  195: 
  196: inline bool manifest_has_key(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
  197:     const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
  198:     return text.find(quoted) != std::string::npos;
  199: }
  200: 
  201: inline std::uint32_t parse_manifest_u32(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
  202:     const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
  203:     std::size_t pos = text.find(quoted);
  204:     if (pos == std::string::npos) {
  205:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest missing key: " + std::string(key));
  206:     }
  207:     pos = text.find(':', pos + quoted.size());
  208:     if (pos == std::string::npos) {
  209:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest malformed key: " + std::string(key));
  210:     }
  211:     ++pos;
  212:     while (pos < text.size() && (text[pos] == ' ' || text[pos] == '\t' || text[pos] == '\r' || text[pos] == '\n')) {
  213:         ++pos;
  214:     }
  215:     std::uint64_t value = 0;
  216:     std::size_t digits = 0;
  217:     while (pos < text.size() && text[pos] >= '0' && text[pos] <= '9') {
  218:         value = value * 10ULL + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(text[pos] - '0');
  219:         if (value > std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max()) {
  220:             throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest key exceeds uint32: " + std::string(key));
  221:         }
  222:         ++pos;
  223:         ++digits;
  224:     }
  225:     if (digits == 0) {
  226:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest key is not uint32: " + std::string(key));
  227:     }
  228:     return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value);
  229: }
  230: 
  231: inline std::uint32_t parse_manifest_u32_any(
  232:     const std::string& text,
  233:     const char* primary_key,
  234:     const char* fallback_key) {
  235:     const bool primary_present = manifest_has_key(text, primary_key);
  236:     const bool fallback_present = manifest_has_key(text, fallback_key);
  237:     if (!primary_present) {
  238:         return parse_manifest_u32(text, fallback_key);
  239:     }
  240:     const std::uint32_t primary = parse_manifest_u32(text, primary_key);
  241:     if (!fallback_present) {
  242:         return primary;
  243:     }
  244:     const std::uint32_t fallback = parse_manifest_u32(text, fallback_key);
  245:     if (primary != fallback) {
  246:         throw std::runtime_error(
  247:             "stream1 manifest conflicting uint32 keys: " + std::string(primary_key) +
  248:             "=" + std::to_string(primary) + " " + std::string(fallback_key) +
  249:             "=" + std::to_string(fallback));
  250:     }
  251:     return primary;
  252: }
  253: 
  254: inline std::string parse_manifest_string(const std::string& text, const char* key) {
  255:     const std::string quoted = std::string("\"") + key + "\"";
  256:     std::size_t pos = text.find(quoted);
  257:     if (pos == std::string::npos) {
  258:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest missing key: " + std::string(key));
  259:     }
  260:     pos = text.find(':', pos + quoted.size());
  261:     if (pos == std::string::npos) {
  262:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest malformed key: " + std::string(key));
  263:     }
  264:     pos = text.find('"', pos + 1);
  265:     if (pos == std::string::npos) {
  266:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest string missing opening quote: " + std::string(key));
  267:     }
  268:     const std::size_t start = pos + 1;
  269:     pos = text.find('"', start);
  270:     if (pos == std::string::npos) {
  271:         throw std::runtime_error("stream1 manifest string missing closing quote: " + std::string(key));
  272:     }
  273:     return text.substr(start, pos - start);
  274: }
  275: 
  276: inline std::string parse_manifest_string_default(
  277:     const std::string& text,
  278:     const char* key,
  279:     const std::string& default_value) {
  280:     try {
  281:         return parse_manifest_string(text, key);
  282:     } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {
  283:         return default_value;
  284:     }
  285: }
  286: 
  287: inline void require_manifest_u32(
  288:     std::uint32_t actual,
  289:     std::uint32_t expected,
  290:     const char* key,
  291:     const std::string& context) {
  292:     if (actual != expected) {
  293:         throw std::runtime_error(
  294:             context + ": stream1 piece_transformer " + key + " must be " + std::to_string(expected) +
  295:             " got " + std::to_string(actual));
  296:     }
  297: }
  298: 
  299: inline void require_manifest_string(
  300:     const std::string& actual,
  301:     const char* expected,
  302:     const char* key,
  303:     const std::string& context) {
  304:     if (actual != expected) {
  305:         throw std::runtime_error(
  306:             context + ": stream1 piece_transformer " + key + " must be " + expected + " got " + actual);
  307:     }
  308: }

  310: inline void validate_mlp_model_config(const Stream1ModelConfig& model, const std::string& context) {
  311:     if (model.state_len != STATE_LEN) {
  312:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 state_len must match compile-time STATE_LEN");
  313:     }
  314:     if (model.num_classes < STATE_LEN ||
  315:         (model.output_dim != MOVE_COUNT && model.output_dim != STREAM1_SINGLE_SCORE_OUTPUT_DIM)) {
  316:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 num_classes/output_dim incompatible with current puzzle");
  317:     }
  318:     if (model.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_FP16 && model.dtype != STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
  319:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 dtype must be fp16 or bf16");
  320:     }
  321:     if (model.normalization != STREAM1_NORM_NONE && model.normalization != STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  322:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 normalization must be none or layernorm");
  323:     }
  324:     if (model.hidden1 == 0U || model.hidden2 == 0U || model.residual_count == 0U) {
  325:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden sizes and residual_count must be nonzero");
  326:     }
  327:     if (model.hidden1 < model.hidden2) {
  328:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden1 must be >= hidden2 because Stream1 reuses hidden1 scratch for residual output");
  329:     }
  330:     if (model.hidden1 % 8U != 0U || model.hidden2 % 8U != 0U) {
  331:         throw std::runtime_error(context + ": stream1 hidden sizes must be multiples of 8 for TensorOp path");
  332:     }
  333: }

  684: inline DeviceWeights upload_weights(const HostWeightBytes& host) {
  685:     DeviceWeights device;
  686:     try {
  687:         if (host.model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  688:             upload_transformer_weights(host, device);
  689:             return device;
  690:         }
  691:         if (host.model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  692:             throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in upload_weights");
  693:         }
  694:         alloc_and_copy(device.input_weight, host.input_weight, "input_weight");
  695:         alloc_and_copy(device.input_bias, host.input_bias, "input_bias");
  696:         if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  697:             alloc_and_copy(device.input_ln_gamma, host.input_ln_gamma, "input_ln_gamma");
  698:             alloc_and_copy(device.input_ln_beta, host.input_ln_beta, "input_ln_beta");
  699:         }
  700:         alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_weight, host.hidden_weight, "hidden_weight");
  701:         alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_bias, host.hidden_bias, "hidden_bias");
  702:         if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  703:             alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_ln_gamma, host.hidden_ln_gamma, "hidden_ln_gamma");
  704:             alloc_and_copy(device.hidden_ln_beta, host.hidden_ln_beta, "hidden_ln_beta");
  705:         }
  706:         device.residual_fc1_weight.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  707:         device.residual_fc1_bias.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  708:         device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  709:         device.residual_fc1_ln_beta.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  710:         device.residual_fc2_weight.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  711:         device.residual_fc2_bias.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  712:         device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  713:         device.residual_fc2_ln_beta.resize(host.model.residual_count);
  714:         for (std::uint32_t block = 0; block < host.model.residual_count; ++block) {
  715:             const std::string prefix = "residual" + std::to_string(block);
  716:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_weight[block], host.residual_fc1_weight[block], (prefix + "_fc1_weight").c_str());
  717:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_bias[block], host.residual_fc1_bias[block], (prefix + "_fc1_bias").c_str());
  718:             if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  719:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma[block], host.residual_fc1_ln_gamma[block], (prefix + "_fc1_ln_gamma").c_str());
  720:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc1_ln_beta[block], host.residual_fc1_ln_beta[block], (prefix + "_fc1_ln_beta").c_str());
  721:             }
  722:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_weight[block], host.residual_fc2_weight[block], (prefix + "_fc2_weight").c_str());
  723:             alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_bias[block], host.residual_fc2_bias[block], (prefix + "_fc2_bias").c_str());
  724:             if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {
  725:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma[block], host.residual_fc2_ln_gamma[block], (prefix + "_fc2_ln_gamma").c_str());
  726:                 alloc_and_copy(device.residual_fc2_ln_beta[block], host.residual_fc2_ln_beta[block], (prefix + "_fc2_ln_beta").c_str());
  727:             }
  728:         }
  729:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_weight_table, device.residual_fc1_weight, "residual_fc1_weight_table");
  730:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_bias_table, device.residual_fc1_bias, "residual_fc1_bias_table");
  731:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_weight_table, device.residual_fc2_weight, "residual_fc2_weight_table");
  732:         alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_bias_table, device.residual_fc2_bias, "residual_fc2_bias_table");
  733:         if (host.model.normalization == STREAM1_NORM_LAYERNORM) {

  735:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc1_ln_beta_table, device.residual_fc1_ln_beta, "residual_fc1_ln_beta_table");
  736:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma_table, device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma, "residual_fc2_ln_gamma_table");
  737:             alloc_managed_pointer_table(device.residual_fc2_ln_beta_table, device.residual_fc2_ln_beta, "residual_fc2_ln_beta_table");
  738:         }
  739:         alloc_and_copy(device.output_weight, host.output_weight, "output_weight");
  740:         alloc_and_copy(device.output_bias, host.output_bias, "output_bias");
  741:         return device;
  742:     } catch (...) {

  747: 
  748: inline void free_weights(DeviceWeights& device) {
  749:     cudaFree(device.input_weight);
  750:     cudaFree(device.input_bias);
  751:     cudaFree(device.input_ln_gamma);
  752:     cudaFree(device.input_ln_beta);
  753:     cudaFree(device.hidden_weight);
  754:     cudaFree(device.hidden_bias);
  755:     cudaFree(device.hidden_ln_gamma);
  756:     cudaFree(device.hidden_ln_beta);
  757:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_weight) {
  758:         cudaFree(ptr);
  759:     }
  760:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_bias) {
  761:         cudaFree(ptr);
  762:     }
  763:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma) {
  764:         cudaFree(ptr);
  765:     }
  766:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc1_ln_beta) {
  767:         cudaFree(ptr);
  768:     }
  769:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_weight) {
  770:         cudaFree(ptr);
  771:     }
  772:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_bias) {
  773:         cudaFree(ptr);
  774:     }
  775:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_ln_gamma) {
  776:         cudaFree(ptr);
  777:     }
  778:     for (half* ptr : device.residual_fc2_ln_beta) {
  779:         cudaFree(ptr);
  780:     }
  781:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_weight_table);
  782:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_bias_table);
  783:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_ln_gamma_table);
  784:     cudaFree(device.residual_fc1_ln_beta_table);

  821: inline void free_stream1_scratch(ScratchAllocation& scratch);
  822: 
  823: inline ScratchAllocation alloc_stream1_scratch(
  824:     const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
  825:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  826:     std::uint32_t lane_count) {
  827:     ScratchAllocation scratch;
  828:     try {
  829:         const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model) * lane_count;
  830:         if (model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  831:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.hidden1, rows * model.hidden1 * sizeof(half)));
  832:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.hidden2, rows * model.hidden2 * sizeof(half)));
  833:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.residual, rows * model.hidden2 * sizeof(half)));
  834:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.output, rows * model.output_dim * sizeof(half)));
  835:             return scratch;
  836:         }
  837:         if (model.backend == STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  838:             const std::uint64_t token_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
  839:             const std::uint64_t qkv_values = rows * model.seq_len * 3ULL * model.d_model;
  840:             const std::uint64_t attention_values = rows * model.nhead * model.seq_len * model.seq_len;
  841:             const std::uint64_t context_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
  842:             const std::uint64_t ff_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.ff_dim;
  843:             const std::uint64_t logits_values = rows * model.output_dim;
  844:             // Future attention kernels can overwrite this float score buffer with softmax probabilities in place.
  845:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_tokens, token_values * sizeof(half)));
  846:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_qkv, qkv_values * sizeof(half)));
  847:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs, attention_values * sizeof(float)));
  848:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_attention_context, context_values * sizeof(half)));
  849:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_ff_hidden, ff_values * sizeof(half)));
  850:             BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&scratch.transformer_logits, logits_values * sizeof(half)));
  851:             return scratch;
  852:         }
  853:         throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in scratch allocation");
  854:     } catch (...) {
  855:         free_stream1_scratch(scratch);
  856:         throw;
  857:     }
  858: }
  859: inline void free_stream1_scratch(ScratchAllocation& scratch) {
  860:     cudaFree(scratch.hidden1);
  861:     cudaFree(scratch.hidden2);
  862:     cudaFree(scratch.residual);
  863:     cudaFree(scratch.output);
  864:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_tokens);
  865:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_qkv);
  866:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_attention_scores_probs);
  867:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_attention_context);
  868:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_ff_hidden);
  869:     cudaFree(scratch.transformer_logits);
  870:     scratch = ScratchAllocation{};
  871: }

  873: inline Stream1NetworkDims network_dims(const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
  874:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP) {
  875:         throw std::runtime_error("piece_transformer Stream1 inference is not implemented in the MLP network view");
  876:     }
  877:     return Stream1NetworkDims{
  878:         model.state_len,
  879:         model.num_classes,
  880:         model.hidden1,
  881:         model.hidden2,
  882:         model.residual_count,
  883:         model.output_dim,
  884:         model.dtype,
  885:         model.normalization};
  886: }

  888: inline std::vector<const half*> const_pointer_vector(const std::vector<half*>& ptrs) {
  889:     return std::vector<const half*>(ptrs.begin(), ptrs.end());
  890: }
  891: inline Stream1TransformerDims transformer_dims(const Stream1ModelConfig& model) {
  892:     if (model.backend != STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER) {
  893:         throw std::runtime_error("MLP Stream1 model cannot be viewed as a piece_transformer");
  894:     }
  895:     return Stream1TransformerDims{
  896:         model.state_len,
  897:         model.num_classes,
  898:         model.num_pieces,
  899:         model.max_piece_size,
  900:         model.seq_len,
  901:         model.d_model,
  902:         model.nhead,
  903:         model.head_dim,
  904:         model.transformer_layers,
  905:         model.ff_dim,
  906:         model.output_dim,
  907:         model.dtype};
  908: }

  974: void stream1_inference_cutlass_cuda(
  975:     const State128* current_frontier_states,

 1168: void stream1_cutlass_linear_cuda(
 1169:     const half* input,
 1170:     const half* weight,
 1171:     half* output,
 1172:     std::uint32_t rows,
 1173:     std::uint32_t input_cols,
 1174:     std::uint32_t output_cols,
 1175:     std::uint32_t dtype,
 1176:     cudaStream_t stream) {
 1177:     NvtxRange range("Stream1_CUTLASS_linear_launch");
 1178: #if BEAM_HAS_CUTLASS
 1179:     cutlass::gemm::GemmCoord problem(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(output_cols), static_cast<int>(input_cols));
 1180:     cutlass::Status status = cutlass::Status::kSuccess;
 1181:     if (dtype == STREAM1_DTYPE_BF16) {
 1182:         int device = 0;
 1183:         cudaDeviceProp prop{};
 1184:         cudaGetDevice(&device);
 1185:         cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device);
 1186:         if (prop.major < 8) {
 1187:             throw std::invalid_argument("Stream1 bf16 weights require SM80+ GPU; use fp16 weights on T4/SM75");
 1188:         }
 1189:         using Gemm = cutlass::gemm::device::Gemm<
 1190:             cutlass::bfloat16_t,
 1191:             cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
 1192:             cutlass::bfloat16_t,
 1193:             cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
 1194:             cutlass::bfloat16_t,
 1195:             cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
 1196:             float,
 1197:             cutlass::arch::OpClassTensorOp,
 1198:             cutlass::arch::Sm80,
 1199:             cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<128, 64, 32>,
 1200:             cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<64, 32, 32>,
 1201:             cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<16, 8, 16>>;
 1202:         Gemm gemm;
 1203:         typename Gemm::Arguments args{
 1204:             problem,
 1205:             {reinterpret_cast<const cutlass::bfloat16_t*>(input), static_cast<int>(input_cols)},
 1206:             {reinterpret_cast<const cutlass::bfloat16_t*>(weight), static_cast<int>(output_cols)},
 1207:             {reinterpret_cast<cutlass::bfloat16_t*>(output), static_cast<int>(output_cols)},
 1208:             {reinterpret_cast<cutlass::bfloat16_t*>(output), static_cast<int>(output_cols)},
 1209:             {1.0f, 0.0f}};
 1210:         status = gemm(args, nullptr, stream);
 1211:     } else {
 1212:         using Gemm = cutlass::gemm::device::Gemm<
 1213:             cutlass::half_t,
 1214:             cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
 1215:             cutlass::half_t,
 1216:             cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
 1217:             cutlass::half_t,
 1218:             cutlass::layout::RowMajor,
 1219:             float,
 1220:             cutlass::arch::OpClassTensorOp,
 1221:             cutlass::arch::Sm75,
 1222:             cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<128, 64, 32>,
 1223:             cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<64, 32, 32>,
 1224:             cutlass::gemm::GemmShape<16, 8, 8>>;
 1225:         Gemm gemm;
 1226:         typename Gemm::Arguments args{
 1227:             problem,
 1228:             {reinterpret_cast<const cutlass::half_t*>(input), static_cast<int>(input_cols)},
 1229:             {reinterpret_cast<const cutlass::half_t*>(weight), static_cast<int>(output_cols)},
 1230:             {reinterpret_cast<cutlass::half_t*>(output), static_cast<int>(output_cols)},
 1231:             {reinterpret_cast<cutlass::half_t*>(output), static_cast<int>(output_cols)},
 1232:             {1.0f, 0.0f}};
 1233:         status = gemm(args, nullptr, stream);
 1234:     }
 1235:     if (status != cutlass::Status::kSuccess) {
 1236:         cudaGetLastError();
 1237:     }
 1238: #else
 1239:     (void)input;
 1240:     (void)weight;
 1241:     (void)output;
 1242:     (void)rows;
 1243:     (void)input_cols;
 1244:     (void)output_cols;
 1245:     (void)dtype;

  527:         fp16_bytes(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(model.output_dim) * model.d_model));
  528:     t.output_bias = read_binary_exact(dir / ("output_bias" + suffix), fp16_bytes(model.output_dim));
  529:     t.piece_positions = read_binary_exact(
  530:         dir / "piece_positions.u16",
  531:         static_cast<std::uint64_t>(model.num_pieces) * model.max_piece_size * sizeof(std::uint16_t));
  532:     t.piece_mask = read_binary_exact(
  533:         dir / "piece_mask.u8",
  534:         static_cast<std::uint64_t>(model.num_pieces) * model.max_piece_size);
  535:     t.piece_types = read_binary_exact(dir / "piece_types.u8", model.num_pieces);
  536:     return weights;
  537: }
  538: 
  539: inline HostWeightBytes load_stream1_weights(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
  540:     const Stream1ModelConfig model = load_stream1_manifest(dir);
  541:     switch (model.backend) {
  542:     case STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP:
  543:         return load_stream1_mlp_weights(dir, model);
  544:     case STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER:
  545:         return load_stream1_transformer_weights(dir, model);
  546:     default:
  547:         throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in weight loader");
  548:     }
  549: }
  550: 
  551: inline std::uint64_t total_host_weight_bytes(const HostWeightBytes& weights) {
  552:     std::uint64_t total = weights.input_weight.size() + weights.input_bias.size() +
  553:         weights.input_ln_gamma.size() + weights.input_ln_beta.size() +
  554:         weights.hidden_weight.size() + weights.hidden_bias.size() +
  555:         weights.hidden_ln_gamma.size() + weights.hidden_ln_beta.size() +
  556:         weights.output_weight.size() + weights.output_bias.size();
  557:     for (std::size_t block = 0; block < weights.residual_fc1_weight.size(); ++block) {
  558:         total += weights.residual_fc1_weight[block].size() + weights.residual_fc1_bias[block].size() +
  559:             weights.residual_fc1_ln_gamma[block].size() + weights.residual_fc1_ln_beta[block].size() +
  560:             weights.residual_fc2_weight[block].size() + weights.residual_fc2_bias[block].size() +
  561:             weights.residual_fc2_ln_gamma[block].size() + weights.residual_fc2_ln_beta[block].size();
  562:     }
  563:     const HostTransformerBytes& t = weights.transformer;
  564:     total += t.fast_slot_projected.size() + t.fast_piece_static.size() + t.cls_token.size() +
  565:         t.input_ln_gamma.size() + t.input_ln_beta.size() + t.output_ln_gamma.size() + t.output_ln_beta.size() +
  566:         t.output_weight.size() + t.output_bias.size() + t.piece_positions.size() + t.piece_mask.size() + t.piece_types.size();
  567:     for (const HostTransformerBlockBytes& b : t.blocks) {
  568:         total += b.ln1_gamma.size() + b.ln1_beta.size() + b.attn_qkv_weight.size() + b.attn_qkv_bias.size() +
  569:             b.attn_out_weight.size() + b.attn_out_bias.size() + b.ln2_gamma.size() + b.ln2_beta.size() +
  570:             b.ff1_weight.size() + b.ff1_bias.size() + b.ff2_weight.size() + b.ff2_bias.size();
  571:     }
  572:     return total;
  573: }
  574: 
  575: inline std::uint64_t transformer_scratch_bytes_for_rows(const Stream1ModelConfig& model, std::uint64_t rows) {
  576:     const std::uint64_t token_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
  577:     const std::uint64_t qkv_values = rows * model.seq_len * 3ULL * model.d_model;
  578:     const std::uint64_t attention_values = rows * model.nhead * model.seq_len * model.seq_len;
  579:     const std::uint64_t context_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.d_model;
  580:     const std::uint64_t ff_values = rows * model.seq_len * model.ff_dim;
  581:     const std::uint64_t logits_values = rows * model.output_dim;
  582:     return fp16_bytes(token_values + qkv_values + context_values + ff_values + logits_values) +
  583:         attention_values * sizeof(float);
  584: }
  585: 
  586: inline std::uint64_t stream1_scratch_bytes(
  587:     const Stream1ModelConfig& model,
  588:     std::uint32_t b_micro,
  589:     std::uint32_t lane_count) {
  590:     const std::uint64_t rows = stream1_inference_rows(b_micro, model) * lane_count;
  591:     switch (model.backend) {
  592:     case STREAM1_BACKEND_MLP:
  593:         return fp16_bytes(rows * (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(model.hidden1) + 2ULL * model.hidden2 + model.output_dim));
  594:     case STREAM1_BACKEND_PIECE_TRANSFORMER:
  595:         return transformer_scratch_bytes_for_rows(model, rows);
  596:     default:
  597:         throw std::runtime_error("unsupported Stream1 backend in scratch byte helper");
  598:     }
  599: }
  600: 
  601: inline std::uint64_t total_device_weight_bytes(const HostWeightBytes& weights) {
  602:     return total_host_weight_bytes(weights);
  603: }
  604: 
  605: #ifndef BEAM_STREAM1_WEIGHT_IO_MANIFEST_ONLY

```

# Stream1 Transformer Forward Verification - 2026-06-29

## Scope
- Added a standalone CUDA Stream1 piece-transformer forward entrypoint without production dispatcher/runner wiring.
- Added separate `Stream1TransformerNetworkView` and `Stream1TransformerScratchView` API surfaces.
- Implemented p900 input token construction from `fast_slot_projected`, `fast_piece_static`, `piece_positions`, and `piece_mask`.
- Implemented input/output LayerNorm, pre-norm transformer blocks, CUTLASS linear projections, SiLU FFN activation, residual adds, and output score-key quantization.
- Implemented a fused row-wise tiled attention kernel for the current `seq_len=51`, using the existing reusable attention score/probability buffer in place, with explicit scratch initialization before the standalone forward. Full external FlashAttention 2 integration is deferred; no new external dependency was added.
- Kept production runner and benchmark fail-closed transformer guards unchanged.

## Red Check

```powershell

## Final Verification

```powershell
docker run --rm --gpus all -v ${PWD}:/workspace -w /workspace gpu-dev-cutlass-nsight:cuda128-sm120 bash -lc "cmake --build build-stream1-transformer-red --target stream1_transformer_cuda_tests -j2 >/tmp/build.log && ./build-stream1-transformer-red/stream1_transformer_cuda_tests"
```

Max score-key error versus fp32 PyTorch reference was 1573 in the final single run, under the 3072 fp16/TensorCore tolerance.  # Changelog
 
 ## 2026-06-29
+- Fixed the benchmark-only Stream1 MLP network view construction after the expanded LayerNorm pointer fields were added. `stream_benchmark` now passes input/hidden LayerNorm pointers and residual LayerNorm pointer tables from loaded device weights, matching the production MLP view shape without changing transformer forward semantics or wiring transformer dispatch. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_forward_2026-06-29.md`.
+- Added the standalone CUDA Stream1 piece-transformer forward path without production dispatcher/runner wiring. The new transformer-specific view/scratch API runs p900 parent rows through fast input token construction, input/output LayerNorm, four pre-norm attention/FFN blocks, CUTLASS linear projections, a fused row-wise seq_len=51 attention kernel, SiLU FFN activation, residual adds, and score-key quantization. Existing MLP/dispatcher tests were updated for the expanded MLP view initializer shape. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_forward_2026-06-29.md`.
 - Hardened the Stream1 piece-transformer loader quality gates. Manifest fallback u32 parsing now only falls back when the primary key is absent and rejects conflicting primary/fallback values, runner and benchmark entry points fail closed before transformer runtime sizing/allocation because forward dispatch is not wired, and Stream1 weight/scratch allocation paths free partial CUDA allocations on exceptions. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_loader_2026-06-29.md`.
 - Added the Stream1 piece-transformer host loader path and scratch arena scaffolding without wiring transformer inference. The weight loader now parses and validates the exact p900 piece-transformer manifest contract, loads exported transformer tensors and piece layout tables with exact byte sizes, exposes host/device/scratch byte helpers, and keeps MLP loading on an explicit separate branch. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_loader_2026-06-29.md`.
 - Added Stream1 piece-transformer export tooling: an auto-detecting tools/export_stream1.py wrapper and tools/export_stream1_transformer.py for Kaggle p900 PilgrimPieceTransformer checkpoints. The exporter writes fast-slot input tables, piece layout tensors, Transformer block tensors, manifests with backend=piece_transformer, rejects non-24-output, non-4-layer, and non-1024-FF variants, and supports optional CPU fp32 reference JSON generation. Verification recorded in test_results/stream1_transformer_exporter_2026-06-29.md.
 - Added tagged Stream1 model backend config constants and transformer metadata fields, preserving existing MLP defaults. Stream1 row-mode helpers now force the piece-transformer backend to parent rows while keeping MLP one-output child-row behavior, with contract coverage recorded in `test_results/stream1_piece_transformer_task1_2026-06-29.md`. Unknown Stream1 backend values now fail closed in row-mode helpers, MLP weight validation, and manifest backend parsing; legacy manifests without `backend` still default to MLP.
 
 ## 2026-06-20
 - Added `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_MODE` for ordinary solve-to-center runs that keep
   searching after the first hit. Stream2 can now record solved candidates
   without setting `stop_flag`; the runner completes the hit depth, completes
   `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_EXTRA_DEPTHS` more depths, gathers solved buckets from all
   ranks, reconstructs every stored solution, and writes per-length summaries.
 - Added `hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket.sh`, a 8-GPU IHES launcher that
   selects known length-23 puzzles from `solv_uniq.csv`, runs original and
   reflected solve-bucket passes, and writes a compact TSV with the best found
   original-facing solution for each variant.
 - Changed resident solution-repair task ordering to process higher puzzle ids
   first. Parsed solution rows are now stably sorted by descending `puzzle_id`,
   so `1000, 999, ...` are prioritized while preserving per-puzzle solution
   order.
 - Added an explicit `BEAM_STREAM1_MODE=uniform` runtime mode for repair/debug
   runs. In this mode Stream1 model inference is not launched; the captured
   ring-slot graph fills the score ring with zero scores so selection behaves as
   an unscored BFS frontier subject only to the configured beam width and
   dedup/final materialization.
 - Fixed distributed repair result reconstruction so rank 0 is always the
   reconstruction controller after a global solved stop. Previously rank 0 could
   write `segment_solved=1` with `segment_len=-1` when another rank owned the
   solved candidate, which made resident repair TSV aggregation report false
   misses or false deltas.
 
 ## 2026-06-13
 - Made the path-trace debugger rank-aware for distributed runs. After each
   local prefix scan, ranks exchange tiny debug packets with NCCL so only the
   rank that owns the tracked prefix continues issuing the next tracked move.
 - Fixed the path-trace prefinal hash scanner to store its temporary block
   indices in the survivor-sized final select scratch instead of the final
   request buffer, which is not a valid scratch target on the multi-rank final
   materialization path.
 - Made MEPhI CMake debug flags in `mephi_8xa100_common.sh` overridable from
   the SLURM environment, so path tracing can be compiled for targeted
   diagnostics without changing the default fast launcher behavior.
 - Added the `+3.0` score offset for Stream1 single-output model quantization so
   models trained with outputs starting at `-3` are shifted into the nonnegative
   runtime score-key range before clamping.
 - Enabled the non-LayerNorm Stream1 CUTLASS runtime path to consume bf16
   exported weights. The post-GEMM bias/ReLU and residual add kernels now use
   dtype-aware scalar loads/stores, while the existing fp16 half2 fast path is
   unchanged.
 - Added an early SM80+ guard for bf16 Stream1 inference so bf16 runs fail
   before kernel launch on GPUs that cannot execute the CUTLASS bf16 path.
 
 ## 2026-06-12
 - Started compile-time puzzle state specialization. CMake now infers
   `BEAM_STATE_LOGICAL_BYTES` from `data/puzzle_info.json` by default, computes
   aligned `BEAM_STATE_PHYSICAL_BYTES`, and passes state size/alignment into all
   targets as compile definitions.
 - Replaced the fixed `State128` storage definition with a configurable
   `StatePacked` alias, while preserving the existing `State128` type name for
   incremental compatibility. Final-response temporary metadata now uses the
   first padding bytes after `STATE_LEN` instead of hard-coded `v[120]`.
 - Made final materialization state buffers align to `alignof(State128)` /
   `alignof(FinalResponse)`, and made the old batchnorm-folded Stream1 exporter
   accept configurable `--num-classes`.
 - Added a CMake-script state-sizing smoke check that verifies the current
   megaminx shape remains `120` logical bytes / `128` physical bytes and that
   exact-alignment state lengths still reserve padding for finalization metadata.
 - Extended compile-time puzzle specialization to infer `BEAM_MOVE_COUNT` from
   `puzzle_info.json`, keeping megaminx at 24 moves and allowing IHES cube to
   compile with 18 moves.
 - Made `production_runner` read generators/move names from either the old
   `actions`/`names` JSON or top-level `generators` JSON, and added
   `BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH` so non-megaminx runs can use `data/puzzle_info.json`
   directly.
 - Added `hpc/ihes_cube_smoke/`, a GitHub-tracked MEPhI SLURM smoke package for
   `cayleypy-ihes-cube`. It downloads Kaggle data, creates dummy 1-output fp16
   weights, compiles with `STATE_LEN=72` / `MOVE_COUNT=18`, and runs a tiny
   single-GPU pipeline check.
 - Standardized tracked HPC SLURM scripts to a `24:00:00` walltime and documented
   dependency-based IHES smoke submission so new checks can wait for existing
   jobs instead of cancelling them.

No code was changed during this diagnostic pass. The observed state was physical shard 7 processing with `clean=3988985`, `dirty=146907`, `existing=4135892`, `available=0`, `raw_count=24057`, and `GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY=0`; likely design gaps are missing remote-receive backpressure and disabled synchronized periodic threshold updates for multi-rank depth flow.
 - Added diagnostic-only fatal trace fields for Stream3 double-buffer overflow: logical shard, selected buffer, sibling physical shard, sibling clean/dirty/processing counts, sibling existing/available capacity, and shard buffer count. No scheduling or backpressure behavior changed.
 - Enabled all existing Kaggle diagnostic logging switches for the next T4x2 run: Stream timing, inference trace, path trace, final validation, final exchange trace, depth logs, debug logs, live rank logs, and runtime `BEAM_DEBUG_PIPELINE_STATS`.
 - Kaggle version 54 reproduced Stream3 fatal code `3002` with expanded diagnostics: both ranks failed in `stream3_remote_recv_collect` at depth 6 after both physical buffers for the target logical shard became `processing=1`. Rank 0 failed on logical shard 2 (`selected=4`, sibling `5`) and rank 1 failed on logical shard 3 (`selected=7`, sibling `6`). Current leading hypothesis: Stream4 can start processing a physical shard that Stream3 previously counted as the writable buffer while a Stream3 ring is still in flight; the local writable-buffer check is not a reservation, and remote receive has no independent ingress reserve. Report saved as `test_results/kaggle_v54_stream3_diagnostics_2026-05-26.md`.
 - Fixed the Stream4 ready-queue A/B scheduling invariant. `stream3_build_ready_shard_queue_kernel` now builds readiness per logical shard, skips any logical shard with an active Stream4 physical buffer, emits at most one physical buffer per logical shard, prioritizes dirty-ready buffers over clean-only buffers during forced flush, and updates `stream3_write_buffer_index` to a non-processing sibling when one has capacity. Added Stream3 CUDA regression tests for logical-shard mutual exclusion, busy-sibling skip, and dirty-priority final flush. Updated `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md` with the logical/physical shard contract: Stream3 is the only Stream4 buffer writer, Stream5 only fills `remote_recv_buffer`, and Stream4 may process at most one A/B physical buffer per logical shard at a time.
 - Disabled Stream3 pre-launch writable-buffer backpressure for multi-rank execution. The dispatcher now keeps the backpressure ready-queue drain only when `WORLD_SIZE == 1`; when `WORLD_SIZE > 1`, Stream3 launches normally and any lack of A/B write capacity remains an explicit fatal overflow instead of being hidden by scheduler throttling. Updated `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md` with this single-GPU-only backpressure rule.
 - Kaggle version 55 confirmed the A/B logical-shard mutex fix changed the failure mode: neither rank had both A/B buffers processing for the failed logical shard. Rank 1 failed with selected physical shard 0 non-processing but nearly full (`existing=4176369`, `available=17935`, `raw_count=24033`) while sibling physical shard 1 was processing; rank 0 failed similarly on logical shard 3. The revealed issue is multi-rank capacity saturation because periodic threshold updates were disabled for `WORLD_SIZE>1`, causing per-rank clean totals around 31M before depth 6 drained.
 - Enabled periodic threshold updates for multi-rank without cross-rank synchronization. Periodic multi-rank updates now build a local completed-shard histogram and update `current_threshold` from that local histogram only; final global threshold still uses NCCL/global aggregation after local final flush.
 - Softened the remaining single-GPU double-buffer backpressure path: if the writable-buffer reserve check fails but no ready Stream4 work exists, the dispatcher logs `stream3_backpressure_bypass` and launches Stream3 instead of throwing a scheduler error. Multi-rank execution still bypasses this pre-launch backpressure entirely.
 - Kaggle version 57 diagnostic run on T4x2 failed with `code=3002` after depth 5. Stream timings show depth 5 wall time about `2.95s`, Stream4 cumulative time about `3.12-3.18s` across two slots, Stream3 ring time about `1.94s`, Stream1/2 time about `1.13s`, and Stream5 time about `0.38s`. Buffer diagnostics show both ranks near `32M` clean resident candidates and `threshold_updates_depth=0` before fatal. Failure occurs during `stream3_remote_recv_collect` before the scheduler gets a chance to update threshold, so the current issue is threshold scheduling/pruning timing rather than the old A/B mutex bug. Report saved as `test_results/kaggle_v57_stream_timing_buffers_2026-05-26.md`.
 - Replaced the previous multi-rank local-only periodic threshold update with a Stream5-owned collective request/update path. Multi-rank dispatch now reflects completed local Stream4 work into `threshold_request_local`, all ranks run an `ncclAllReduce(Max)` request check at Stream5 exchange points, and any rank request triggers a global histogram allreduce plus monotonic threshold update on all ranks before remote receive collection. The dispatcher refreshes completed Stream4 slots immediately before the Stream5 request check so jobs that finish while waiting for Stream3 are counted. Added double-buffered `current_threshold[2]`, `threshold_initialized[2]`, and `current_threshold_active_index`; Stream5 writes the inactive slot and commits by active-index update afte

The recorded requirement was “fused/tiled attention for seq_len=51 without adding FA2” plus a later note to use FA2 or similar where applicable. The implementation is a small fixed tile kernel with global score/prob scratch; that is not production-grade FA2, but given this commit’s standalone, p900-only scope I’m treating it as an important performance caveat only if it violates an explicit acceptance gate.
```

The math/order/scratch path is coherent for the stated p900 standalone scope: input construction only reads logical state bytes via exported `piece_positions`/`piece_mask`, attention uses deterministic fixed seq/head loops, scratch is explicitly zeroed before use, and score quantization skips invalid rows.
